# Graph Report - jcode  (2026-08-26)

## Corpus Check
- 1858 files · ~5,587,783 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 40671 nodes · 99797 edges · 1458 communities (1349 shown, 109 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6905 edges (avg confidence: 0.79)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `fcb111c6`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
